Building upon the foundation of the EZSMT+ system, EZSMTV3 introduces a more expressive input language, supports optimization via weak constraints, and offers foundations for streamlined integration of new constraint types. Rather than implementing custom search procedures, EZSMTV3 leverages state-of-the-art SMT solvers, such as CVC5, YICES, and Z3 to perform reasoning. The paper provides benchmarking results comparing EZSMTV3 with its CASP peers such as CLINGCON, CLINGO[DL], and CLINGO[LP], while showcasing its ability to handle mixed-domain constraints involving both integers and reals. The system provides a robust platform for future extensions and theoretical exploration within the CASP domain.

</details>


### [106] [Set-shifting Behavioral Test for Harnessed Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13396)
*Ziwei Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体在工具可靠性发生隐蔽变化时的适应能力，借鉴认知心理学中的"定势转换"概念，通过构建冗余工具库和分支调度框架来评估智能体的工具选择行为，发现智能体会形成固定模式且转换准确率有限，同时工具集的呈现方式会影响路由动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在持续会话中，当可靠工具发生隐蔽变化时，其工具选择行为会发生什么变化，即智能体对隐蔽可靠性变化的适应能力。

Method: 从认知心理学借用"定势转换"概念；构建带有冗余性的工具-技能库（多个工具可解决相同任务但隐蔽可靠性不同）；采用分支调度框架，在隐蔽边界处转换可靠工具组，并配对无转换对照；使用定势转换准确率（每个转换后窗口内路由到目标工具组的联合概率）作为评估指标；在开源智能体框架中测试开源LLM。

Result: 默认情况下，智能体在每个边界后的几轮内会形成小幅重复常规，调用份额集中在几个离散值上；不同智能体在相同常规集上呈现出性质不同的失败模式；工具集的呈现方式（将备选工具视为竞争关系还是互补关系）会改变路由动态。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在面对隐蔽可靠性变化时适应能力有限，容易固化为固定的工具选择模式，且工具集的框架设计对路由行为有显著影响。

Abstract: What happens to an LLM agent's tool choice when the reliable tool silently changes within an ongoing session? We borrow set-shifting from cognitive psychology to study how well agents adapt to hidden reliability shifts. Our benchmark mounts tool-skill libraries with redundancies, where many tools solve the same task but differ in hidden reliability. In our evaluation framework, a branched schedule shifts the reliable tool group at hidden boundaries and pairs every shift with a no-shift control. We find that agents, by default, settle on a small recurring routine within a few turns of each boundary, with call shares concentrating on a few discrete values after each reliability shift. We score the set-shifting accuracy for each agent trajectory: the joint probability of routing to the target tool group in every post-shift window. We test open-weight LLMs in an open-source agentic harness and find qualitatively distinct failure modes across the same set of routines. We also find that set framing, how the toolset presents the alternatives as competing or complementary, shifts the routing dynamics.

</details>


### [107] [LAPO: Leave-One-Turn Attribution for Self-Generated Process Rewards in Multi-Turn Search Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13501)
*Qiang Zhu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAPO是一种基于反向留一回合归因的自生成过程监督方法，通过衡量将某回合替换为[DELETE]占位符后策略对正确答案的平均对数似然变化（即答案似然增益）来评估每个搜索回合的贡献，无需额外奖励模型或验证器，在七个知识密集型问答数据集上平均精确匹配得分为0.326，超越强基线IGPO达0.053。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮搜索推理的强化学习通常仅依赖最终结果奖励，无法区分中间交互的有用性、冗余性和有害性，需要一种无需额外监督信号即可对中间步骤进行细粒度评估的过程监督方法。

Method: 提出LAPO方法，对每个搜索回合，将其及检索观察替换为固定[DELETE]占位符，测量当前策略在替换前后对金标准答案的平均对数似然变化（答案似然增益），以此估计回合贡献；进一步应用符号一致性门控，仅保留原始归因分数与归一化过程优势方向一致的信号。

Result: 在七个知识密集型问答数据集上，LAPO的平均精确匹配得分为0.326，超过最强步骤奖励基线IGPO（0.273）达0.053。消融实验表明反向归因和符号一致性门控具有互补效益。

Conclusion: 基于策略的回顾性归因能够为多轮搜索智能体提供有效的过程监督，且无需额外奖励模型、教师模型、验证器或大模型作为评判者。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multi-turn search reasoning typically relies on terminal outcome rewards, which cannot distinguish useful, redundant, and harmful intermediate interactions. We propose LAPO, a self-generated process-supervision method based on backward leave-one-turn attribution. For each search turn, LAPO replaces the turn and its retrieval observation with a fixed [DELETE] placeholder and measures the resulting change in the current policy's mean log-likelihood of the gold answer. This Answer-Likelihood Gain estimates the turn's contribution while preserving all downstream interactions, allowing early evidence to be evaluated in the complete reasoning context. LAPO further applies sign-consistency gating, retaining only normalized process advantages whose directions agree with their raw attribution scores. The method requires no additional reward model, teacher, verifier, or LLM-as-a-Judge. Across seven knowledge-intensive question-answering datasets with local retrieval, LAPO achieves an average exact-match score of 0.326, outperforming the strongest step-reward baseline, IGPO, by 0.053. Ablations show complementary benefits from backward attribution and sign-consistency gating, demonstrating that policy-derived retrospective attribution can provide effective process supervision for multi-turn search agents.

</details>


### [108] [How Far Can Root Cause Analysis Go on Real-World Telemetry Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13548)
*Athira Gopal,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化多智能体根因分析流水线，在OpenRCA数据集上显著优于现有方法。通过逆向推理代理发现，大多数故障的根本原因证据已存在于数据中，瓶颈在于智能体的推理能力而非数据获取。推理性能受限于模型能力，单纯改进工程和数据管道无法弥补这一差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境微服务故障的根因分析需要处理大规模、多模态的遥测数据（指标、日志、轨迹），传统方法和基于LLM的方法在此任务上表现不佳，OpenRCA数据集展示了这一挑战：所有现有方法准确率均较低。

Method: 提出结构化多智能体RCA流水线，支持领域知识和无知识两种模式；引入逆向推理代理，通过给定正确答案回溯分析证据是否存在于提取的异常信号中，将故障分类为推理缺口（证据存在但未使用）或数据模糊性（证据确实缺失）；提出自动化规则挖掘流水线，从逆向推理报告中系统提取判别规则。

Result: 结构化多智能体RCA流水线大幅优于现有LLM-based和经典基线方法；逆向推理分析显示绝大多数故障中所需证据已经存在，瓶颈是推理能力而非数据获取；自动化规则挖掘减少了对手动领域知识的依赖。

Conclusion: 模型推理能力和领域知识是主要约束因素；更强的模型嵌入更多领域专业知识，显式知识注入可部分弥补这一差距；即使证据提取完美，推理性能仍存在实际上限，单纯改进脚手架工程和数据管道无法解决，需要在模型层面取得进展。

Abstract: Identifying root causes in production microservice failures requires reasoning over large-scale, multimodal telemetry spanning metrics, logs, and traces, a problem that has proved resistant to both classical and LLM-based approaches. The OpenRCA dataset exemplifies these challenges: it is large-scale, multimodal, and lacks detailed domain knowledge, and yields consistently low accuracy across all existing methods. We show that classical causal discovery methods and existing LLM-based multi-agent systems fail to reliably identify root causes on this benchmark, and present a Structured Multi-Agent RCA pipeline that substantially outperforms existing LLM-based and classical baselines, supporting both domain-knowledge and knowledge-free operating modes. To diagnose where failures originate, we introduce a reverse reasoning agent that, given the correct answer, identifies which signals in the extracted anomalies support it and determines whether Stage~1 had access to those signals, classifying each failure as Reasoning Gap (evidence present but unused) or Data Ambiguity (evidence genuinely absent). This analysis reveals that the required evidence is present in the vast majority of failures: the bottleneck is not data access but the agent's ability to reason over it correctly. We further introduce an automated rule mining pipeline that systematically extracts discrimination rules from reverse reasoning reports, reducing reliance on manual knowledge curation. Across all configurations, model reasoning capability and domain knowledge are the primary constraints: stronger models embed more domain expertise, and explicit knowledge injection partially compensates for this gap. Reasoning performance remains practically bounded even when evidence extraction is perfect: scaffold engineering and better data pipelines alone cannot close this gap; progress requires improvements at the model level.

</details>


### [109] [Multi-Agent Collaborative Reasoning with Tool-Augmented Evidence for Urban Region Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13558)
*Xixuan Hao,Yutian Jiang,Jiabo Liu,Yihang Yang,Guangyin Jin,Song Gao,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UrbanAgent是一个将城市区域概况分析重构为推理驱动问题的智能体框架，通过多智能体协作推理、主动证据获取和迭代推理，在碳排放、GDP和人口估计任务上平均R²提升8.1%，并在未见城市场景中表现强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市区域概况分析方法大多基于多模态表示学习，存在三个关键局限：一是相关性驱动而非因果推理；二是假设跨模态数据一致性，无法处理异质性；三是依赖静态pipeline，鲁棒性不足。因此需要一种能够主动推理、处理模态不一致的方法。

Method: UrbanAgent提出智能体框架：(1) 为每种数据模态实例化独立智能体；(2) 进行结构化多智能体协作推理，明确处理跨模态不一致性而非合并为单一表示；(3) 将指标预测扩展为闭环过程，包含主动证据获取和迭代推理；(4) 通过工具增强的外部知识检索进行不确定性验证，并利用强化学习优化。

Result: 在全球城市数据集上的碳排放、GDP和人口估计实验中，UrbanAgent一致优于现有基线，平均R²提升8.1%，且在未见过城市场景中展现强泛化性能。

Conclusion: 将城市区域概况从相关性驱动的表示学习转向推理驱动的多智能体协作范式，能够有效处理跨模态不一致性并提升预测性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Urban region profiling constitutes a core problem in urban computing, supporting applications such as population estimation, economic assessment, and environmental monitoring. Existing methods typically formulate this task as multimodal representation learning, fusing heterogeneous urban data, e.g., satellite imagery, points of interest, textual descriptions, and 3D building information, into latent embeddings for prediction. However, these approaches are largely correlation-driven, assume cross-modal consistency, and rely on static pipelines, which limit their robustness in heterogeneous or unseen urban regions. We propose UrbanAgent, an agentic framework that reframes urban region profiling as a reasoning-driven inference problem. UrbanAgent instantiates an independent agent for each data modality and performs structured multi-agent collaborative reasoning to explicitly address cross-modal inconsistencies rather than absorbing them into a single representation. In addition, UrbanAgent extends indicator prediction as a closed-loop process of active evidence acquisition and iterative reasoning, enabling agents to verify uncertain inferences through tool-augmented retrieval of external knowledge optimized via reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments on global urban datasets for Carbon emissions, GDP, and Population estimation show that UrbanAgent consistently outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average improvement of 8.1% in R2, and exhibiting strong generalization performance in unseen-city settings.

</details>


### [110] [AI advice suppresses people's willingness to say "I don't know", even when the advice is wrong and accuracy is incentivized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13562)
*Chiara Marcoccia,Walter Quattrociocchi,Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过五项实验（N=3,132）发现，AI建议（即使错误）会显著降低人们暂停判断的意愿，导致答案正确率下降约三分之二，而信心却近乎翻倍。经济激励可部分缓解这一效应，但无法完全恢复无AI状态下的判断暂停水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手几乎对任何问题都能提供流畅的回答，但人类具备"我不知道"这一元认知判断能力。研究旨在探究AI建议是否会改变人们判断自己是否足够知道答案的元认知阈值。

Method: 五项实验（四项预先注册，一项直接复制），共3,132名被试。参与者回答困难问题并始终可以选择不回答。问题经过设计使AI建议错误，从而分离AI使用与准确性。实验操纵了AI是否可用、建议是主动请求还是被动显示，以及准确性的经济激励条件。

Result: 1) 仅拥有AI访问权限就几乎消除了参与者暂停判断的意愿（无论建议是主动请求还是被动显示）。2) 因此参与者回答了更多问题，但正确率约为无AI时的三分之一，而信心却几乎翻倍。3) 激励准确性并惩罚不准确性可减少对AI建议的寻求和遵从，提高答案准确性并增加暂停判断，但仍远低于无AI时的水平。

Conclusion: 随着AI建议变得无处不在且常常未经请求，它们不仅影响答案的准确性，还可能改变人们决定自己是否足够知道答案的元认知阈值。

Abstract: Knowing when to say "I don't know" is fundamental to human judgment, yet AI assistants offer a fluent answer to almost any question. In five experiments (N = 3,132; four preregistered, one direct replication), participants answered difficult questions and could always decline to respond. We engineered the questions so that AI advice was wrong, separating AI use from its accuracy. Merely having access to AI nearly eliminated participants' willingness to suspend judgment, and this held whether the advice was actively requested or simply displayed. Consequently, participants answered more questions but were correct about a third as often as when AI was unavailable-yet their confidence nearly doubled. Incentivizing accuracy and penalizing inaccuracy led participants to seek and follow AI advice less, answer more accurately, and suspend judgment more often, though still far less than when AI was unavailable. As AI suggestions grow ubiquitous and unsolicited, they may not simply affect answer accuracy; they may even alter the metacognitive threshold at which people decide whether they know enough to answer.

</details>


### [111] [SAFETY SENTRY: Context-Aware Human Intervention via EXECUTE-ASK-REFUSE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13594)
*Tianyu Chen,Chujia Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Safety Sentry，一种轻量级LLM智能体安全守卫模型，将传统二元安全判断重构为{执行、询问、拒绝}三选一路由决策，通过单次解码调用和动态阈值实现跨部署风险容忍度的灵活调整，在准确率和安全召回率上优于多种开源和闭源基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二元安全守卫模型将"动作本身是否有害"和"在用户上下文中是否恰当"两个不同决策混为一谈，且基于动作类别而非实例粒度进行判断，导致频繁中断、削弱用户自主性、降低告警敏感性。

Method: 将安全判断重构为每个实例的三选一路由决策{EXECUTE, ASK, REFUSE}，采用轻量级守卫模型Safety Sentry，推理简化为单次解码调用，通过单次解码时阈值调整即可在不同风险容忍度的部署间迁移，无需重新训练。

Result: Safety Sentry在总体准确率和安全相关召回率上优于多种开源权重和前沿闭源基线模型，同时能够控制两个方向上的错误率。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度的实例级三路决策和轻量级架构，可以有效提升LLM智能体工具调用的安全性，同时兼顾用户体验和跨部署的灵活适配。

Abstract: LLM agents act on real-world environments through tool calls, and a single misjudged action can cause irreversible harm. The standard safeguard is a guard model that labels each proposed action as safe or unsafe, but this binary view conflates two distinct decisions: whether the action is harmful in itself, and whether it is appropriate given the user's context. It also operates at the granularity of action categories rather than individual instances, producing routine interruptions that erode autonomy and train users to wave through the most consequential alerts. We reframe the problem as a per-instance three-way routing decision over {EXECUTE, ASK, REFUSE} and instantiate it with Safety Sentry, a lightweight guard model whose inference reduces to a single decoding call. A single decoding-time threshold lets one fixed checkpoint be re-positioned across deployments of differing risk tolerance without retraining. Safety Sentry outperforms a broad set of open-weight and frontier closed-source baselines on overall accuracy and safety-related recall, while controlling both directional error rates simultaneously.

</details>


### [112] [Automatic Ordinary Differential Equations Discovery For Biological Systems Using Large Language Model Powered Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13608)
*David Krongauz,Arad Zulti,Eran Segal,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MEDA系统是一个结合大语言模型和符号回归的代理框架，能够自动发现生物动力系统的常微分方程模型，在检索、外推和开放式发现任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么聚焦于窄域方程发现基准，要么关注端到端自动化流水线，而生物系统领域相对未被充分探索。作者希望构建一个能够自主发现生物动力系统ODE模型的智能框架。

Method: MEDA系统集成了大语言模型和符号回归，通过检索背景知识、定义可容许变量、生成机制约束、提出候选ODE并进行拟合与评估的完整流程来发现生物动力系统模型。

Result: 在典范模型检索、基于推理的外推到未见变体以及开放式发现任务中，MEDA成功恢复了正确的状态变量，实现了强结构恢复能力，并生成了生物学上合理的发现导向模型。

Conclusion: 知识引导的形式化与机制约束是关键组件；仅靠数值拟合可能保留轨迹兼容但生物学上不正确的方程，说明先验知识在生物模型发现中的重要性。

Abstract: Automatic scientific discovery has long been a goal of computational scholars - a machine that can discover nature's secrets on its own, moving computational systems beyond data-fitting tools toward the generation and refinement of mechanistic models of the universe. Recent advances in symbolic regression (SR) and large-language-model (LLM)-based agents suggest that such systems can recover equations from data, incorporate domain priors, and automate parts of the research workflow. However, most existing approaches either focus on narrow equation-discovery benchmarks or broad end-to-end automation pipelines, while biological systems remain comparatively underexplored. Here, we introduce the MEDA system, an LLM- and SR-powered agentic framework for discovering ordinary-differential-equation (ODE) models of biological and biologically inspired dynamical systems. MEDA retrieves background knowledge, defines admissible variables, generates mechanistic constraints, proposes candidate ODEs, and fits and evaluates them. We evaluate it across canonical model retrieval, reasoning-based extrapolation to unseen variants, and open-ended discovery, with and without experimental data. Across these settings, MEDA recovered the correct state variables, achieved strong structural recovery in retrieval and extrapolation tasks, and produced biologically plausible discovery-oriented models. Ablation and robustness analyses show that knowledge-guided formalization and mechanistic constraints are load-bearing components, whereas numerical fitting alone can preserve trajectory-compatible but biologically incorrect equations.

</details>


### [113] [STOCKTAKE: Measuring the Gap Between Perception and Action in LLM Agents with a Fair Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13618)
*Sagar Deb,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STOCKTAKE基准，用于评估LLM agent在部分可观测的供应链补货任务中的状态估计与控制能力。实验发现，四个前沿模型能检测84-88%的隐藏故障，但技能得分差异巨大（0.62到-0.23），低于基线水平。失败表现为两种形式：诊断正确但未采取行动（知道-行动差距），以及过度反应导致成本超过保护收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent评估无法区分两种失败模式：agent可能错误理解世界状态，或正确理解但未能采取适当行动（知道-行动差距）。现有参考策略要么使用agent无法获取的特权信息，要么完全缺失，无法公平评估agent的状态估计能力。

Method: 构建26周的供应链补货基准STOCKTAKE，采用因子化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），包含六个隐藏因子过程。设计可计算的公平参考策略：每个因子使用精确贝叶斯滤波器驱动rollout策略，与agent接收相同观测流。通过技能得分（0为症状盲基线，1为oracle）量化表现，通过每周书面推理检测状态估计延迟和知道-行动率。

Result: Claude Sonnet 5、GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-V4-Pro和Grok 4.5能检测84-88%的隐藏故障，通常在故障发生后一周内发现。但技能得分范围从0.62到-0.23，其中两个模型低于症状盲基线。在持续压力下，34-43%的正确诊断周仍导致缺货。低于基线的两个模型在诊断周缺货率最低，表明不足响应只是失败的一面，另一面是过度响应成本超过保护收益。

Conclusion: 当前LLM agent在供应链任务中存在显著的知道-行动差距，且失败具有双向性：既可能响应不足（诊断正确但缺货），也可能响应过度（成本超过保护收益）。STOCKTAKE能够测量这两个方向的失败。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly evaluated on multi-week decision tasks in which the state that drives cost is never directly observed. On such tasks the final cost cannot say why an agent failed: it may have misread the world, or read it correctly and still failed to act (the knowing-doing gap). Existing evaluations cannot separate these two failures; their reference policies either read privileged information the agent never sees, or are missing altogether. We introduce STOCKTAKE, a 26-week supply-chain replenishment benchmark built as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with six hidden factor processes, designed so that a fair reference policy is computable: an exact Bayes filter per factor drives a rollout policy on the identical observation stream the agent receives. Scoring each run between a symptom-blind base-stock floor (0) and this oracle (1) yields a skill score, and grading each week's written rationale yields a stated-belief detection lag and a knowing-doing rate, so state estimation and control are measured separately. On fifty seeds with curated stress profiles, Claude Sonnet 5, GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Grok 4.5 detect 84-88% of hidden failures, typically within a week of onset, yet span skill scores from 0.62 to -0.23: two of the four end below the symptom-blind floor while naming factors slightly faster than the two that beat it. The failure has two faces. Where stress persists, 34-43% of correctly diagnosed stress weeks still end in stockout for every model, a rate that partly reflects the severity of the weeks models notice. That rate also runs opposite to skill: the two models under the floor stock out least on diagnosed weeks, so under-response is only one face of the gap, and their traces point to the other, responses whose cost exceeds what they protect. STOCKTAKE measures both directions of that failure.

</details>


### [114] [UESF-Bench: Benchmarking and Probing for Unified Embodied Seeking and Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13621)
*Kun Yu,Jianhua Yang,Yixiang Chen,Changwei Wang,Hongyuan Yu,Yan Huang,Fushuo Huo,Ya Jing,Zhumin Chen,Keji He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UESF-Bench统一基准和SeekFollow-VLA框架，解决具身智能体在动态环境中从语言引导搜索到持续跟踪的统一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准假设目标人物在任务开始时可见，忽略了智能体需要先搜索语言描述的目标再持续跟踪的真实需求；且搜索和跟踪通常被作为分离任务处理，缺乏统一评估基准。

Method: 构建大规模多样化UESF-Bench基准，提出SeekFollow-VLA视觉-语言-动作框架，采用任务驱动路由机制实现搜索与跟踪之间的潜在状态推理与转换建模。

Result: SeekFollow-VLA在单人及多人环境中均优于单头与双头基线方法，建立了统一具身搜索与跟踪的基线。

Conclusion: UESF-Bench和SeekFollow-VLA有效弥合了搜索与跟踪任务之间的鸿沟，为具身智能体在动态环境中实现语言引导的搜索-跟踪统一能力提供了评估基准和有效方法。

Abstract: Language-guided human following is an important capability for embodied agents, but existing benchmarks typically assume that the target person is visible at the start of an episode. This setting simplifies the problem and overlooks a more realistic requirement: an agent often needs to first find a language-described target and then persistently follow that target in a dynamic environment. While recent work has started to study human search, existing settings are typically evaluated in task-specific scenarios and often rely on stronger prior knowledge of the environment. Moreover, they usually treat searching and following as separate tasks and still lack a unified benchmark for systematic evaluation. To address these limitations, we introduce the Unified Embodied Seeking and Following Benchmark (UESF-Bench), a large-scale and diverse benchmark for embodied human seeking and following. The benchmark requires agents to handle semantic-guided exploration, reliable behavior switching and recovery, and delayed identity grounding. To this end, we propose SeekFollow-VLA, a vision-language-action framework with a task-driven routing mechanism for latent phase inference and transition modeling between seeking and following. Experimental results show that SeekFollow-VLA achieves clear improvements over both single-head and dual-head baselines across single-person and multi-person environments, establishing a baseline for unified embodied seek-and-follow.

</details>


### [115] [Explaining Reinforcement Learning Agents via Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13655)
*Celeste Veronese,Edoardo Zorzi,Daniele Meli,Alessandro Farinelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于逻辑的、面向规划的可解释性度量指标（激活率、特征覆盖度、句法距离、语义距离），用于量化强化学习策略的符号化解释程度，并在单/多智能体场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释强化学习（XRL）主要依赖用户研究，缺乏共享的客观评估指标；而逻辑方法虽能提供可读的抽象表示，但缺乏系统量化可解释性的方法。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用归纳逻辑编程（ILP）提取RL策略的符号化表示，定义了四类可解释性度量：激活率（规则与行为的对齐程度）、特征覆盖度（特征在决策中的作用）、句法距离与语义距离（策略在训练中和跨智能体的演化）。

Result: 实验表明，所提度量能揭示动作层面的学习动态、提供比传统全局特征重要性方法更细粒度的特征洞察，并捕捉多智能体强化学习中的协作、专门化和适应模式，同时为策略的迁移与泛化提供关键见解。

Conclusion: 本工作为XRL提供了客观、可量化的可解释性评估框架，同时推进了逻辑XAI领域中可解释性的形式化度量方法，超越了常识性评估和简单命题逻辑片段。

Abstract: Explainable Reinforcement Learning (XRL) seeks to make Reinforcement Learning (RL) policies more transparent and interpretable, a key requirement in safety-critical and human-centric scenarios. However, it is mostly based on user studies, thus targeting the needs of a specific audience and lacking shared evaluation metrics. On the other hand, logic-based approaches within eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) provide compact, human-readable abstractions of decision-making. However, the systematic quantification of the explainability degree of logical representations remains an open problem. This work aims to advance the state of the art in XRL by introducing objective and planning-oriented metrics for policy explainability in RL settings. At the same time, it contributes to the field of logic for XAI by providing a principled way to quantify the explainability of logical rules, moving beyond common-sense assessments and simple propositional fragments. We employ Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) to extract symbolic representations of RL policies and define a novel set of explainability metrics, including activation rate, feature coverage, syntactic distance and semantic distance. These metrics quantify alignment between symbolic rules and agent behavior, the role of features in decision-making, and the evolution of policies during training and across agents in single and multi-agent RL. Experiments across different RL domains show that the proposed metrics highlight action-specific learning dynamics beyond global return, provide fine-grained insights into domain features beyond classical approaches for global feature importance estimation, and uncover coordination, specialization, and adaptation patterns in MARL. Moreover, they provide crucial insights for the transfer and generalization of action-specific policies.

</details>


### [116] [When Bots Join the Team: Bot Adoption and the Institutional Fabric of Open-Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13679)
*Yongren Shi,Wenyi Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI机器人加入开源软件团队后对群体组织的影响。通过分析2,991个GitHub项目在首次引入机器人前后两年的数据，发现机器人加入后带来了更多重复协作、对特定机器人的更高识别、更少的冲突级联以及更独特的输出。这些变化集中在机器人加入时点附近，而非逐渐积累。研究认为可预测、基于规则的智能体可以成为社区社会基础设施的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当自动化智能体成为团队的常规参与者时，群体组织是增强还是削弱。

Method: 在开源软件环境中，将机器人视为参与者而非工具，分析2,991个GitHub项目在首次引入机器人前后各两年的数据。测量制度理论中与持久协调相关的三种能力（重复参与、社会记忆、角色分化）以及两种结果（冲突级联和输出独特性）。

Result: 机器人采用后出现更多重复协作、对特定机器人的更大认可、更少冲突级联以及更独特的输出。变化集中在采用时点附近而非逐渐积累。能力根据其功能（协调vs分化）预测结果，而非由人类或机器人提供；人类侧能力解释了机器人-冲突关联但未解释机器人-独特性关联。

Conclusion: 可预测、基于规则的智能体可以成为社区社会基础设施的一部分。机器人是契机，社会组织是机制。

Abstract: AI agents are joining human teams, raising a basic question: when an automated agent becomes a regular participant, does group organization strengthen or weaken? We study this question in open-source software, where bots open pull requests, review code, and merge changes alongside people, leaving a public record of every interaction. Treating bots as participants rather than tools, we examine 2,991 GitHub projects for two years before and after each adopted its first bot. We measure three capabilities that institutional theory links to durable coordination - repeated engagement, social memory, and role differentiation - and two outcomes: conflict cascades and output distinctiveness. Bot adoption is followed by more repeated collaboration, greater recognition of specific bots in discussion, fewer conflict cascades, and more distinctive outputs. These changes cluster around adoption rather than accumulating gradually. Because we lack an untreated comparison group, we interpret the results as precisely timed associations, not causal effects. Two patterns are difficult for alternative explanations to account for: capabilities predict outcomes according to their function - coordination versus differentiation - rather than whether humans or bots provide them, and human-side capabilities account for the bot-conflict association but not the bot-distinctiveness association. The findings are consistent with a specific interpretation: predictable, rule-based agents can become part of a community's social infrastructure. The bot is the occasion; social organization is the mechanism.

</details>


### [117] [AgentCompass: A Unified Evaluation Infrastructure for Agent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13705)
*Zichen Ding,Jiaye Ge,Shufan Jiang,Kai Chen,Mo Li,Qingqiu Li,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tiaohao Liang,Shudong Liu,Zerun Ma,Zixing Shang,Wenhui Tian,Zun Wang,Liwei Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Jun Xu,Bowen Yang,Dingbo Yuan,Qi Zhang,Songyang Zhang,Peiheng Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentCompass是一个开源、轻量级、可扩展的LLM智能体评估基础设施，通过解耦基准测试、引擎和环境三个独立组件，支持异步容错运行和轨迹分析，原生覆盖20+基准测试和5个能力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体评估管线高度碎片化、紧耦合，阻碍了可重复性并导致大量冗余工程，亟需统一且灵活的评估基础设施。

Method: 将评估过程解耦为Benchmark、Harness、Environment三个独立组件，实现灵活配置无需重写执行逻辑；设计容错异步运行时和全面轨迹分析工具以透明诊断奖励黑客等细粒度失败模式。

Result: 原生支持20+基准测试，覆盖五个能力维度，提供了可扩展且可重复的评估基础设施。

Conclusion: AgentCompass通过解耦架构和全面工具链，有效解决了当前智能体评估中的碎片化和可重复性问题，为社区提供了统一的评估方案。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents, the need for unified evaluation infrastructure becomes critical. However, current evaluation pipelines remain highly fragmented and tightly coupled, hindering reproducibility and causing redundant engineering. To address this, we introduce AgentCompass, an open-source, lightweight, and extensible infrastructure for evaluating LLM-based agents. AgentCompass organizes the evaluation process around three independent components, namely Benchmark, Harness, and Environment, thereby enabling flexible configurations without requiring the reimplementation of complex execution logic. Furthermore, it features a fault-tolerant asynchronous runtime and comprehensive trajectory analysis tools to transparently diagnose nuanced failure modes like reward-hacking. Natively supporting over 20 benchmarks across five capability dimensions, AgentCompass provides the community with a scalable and reproducible infrastructure for advancing agent research.

</details>


### [118] [CAVA: Canonical Action Verification and Attestation for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13716)
*Zexun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAVA是一个运行时语义层，将异构代理AI系统的活动转换为规范化的运行时动作对象，为PCAA治理流程提供稳定的底层动作表征，解决跨运行时环境的动作审批、证据绑定和可验证性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI系统运行在异构运行时环境（本地编码钩子、SDK工具、浏览器自动化、托管代理轨迹、API网关、工作流引擎）中，一个操作行为被多种不兼容的运行时记录表示，导致基本的治理问题难以回答：实际批准了什么动作、什么证据将批准与执行绑定、独立验证者能否复现同一动作身份。

Method: CAVA通过规范动作身份、语义模式检测、审批绑定、收据完整性、运行时可移植投影和可选证明子层，将异构运行时活动转换为规范的运行时动作对象。位于PCAA（部署者拥有的路由-审查-证明治理流程）之下，为其提供稳定的治理对象。

Result: 通过96种子、384变体的基准测试，涵盖语义等价性、语义分离、包装器绕过、假阳性控制、审批绑定、收据可复现性、证明篡改检测、运行时可移植性、语义模式检测、策略退化和Azure部署演练等维度。

Conclusion: 动作级规范化和策略可寻址的语义模式是部署方AI治理的必要基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly act through heterogeneous runtimes: local coding hooks, SDK tools, browser automation, managed-agent traces, API gateways, and workflow engines. A single operational act such as publishing code, changing identity state, moving money, or exporting data may therefore be represented by many incompatible runtime records. This makes a basic governance question difficult to answer: what action was actually approved, what evidence binds the approval to execution, and can an independent verifier reproduce the same action identity later?
  This paper presents Canonical Action Verification and Attestation (CAVA), a runtime-semantics layer for converting heterogeneous agent activity into canonical runtime action objects. CAVA is positioned below Proof-Carrying Agent Actions (PCAA): PCAA defines the deployer-owned route-review-prove governance process, while CAVA defines the stable action object that process governs. The paper formalizes canonical action identity, semantic pattern detection, approval binding, receipt integrity, runtime-portable projection, and optional attestation substrates. We study a reference implementation through a 96-seed, 384-variant benchmark covering semantic equivalence, semantic separation, wrapper bypass, false-positive control, approval binding, receipt reproducibility, attestation tamper detection, runtime portability, semantic pattern detection, policy degradation, and Azure deployment drills. The contribution is a systems formulation of action-level canonicalization and policy-addressable semantic patterns as a necessary substrate for deployer-side AI governance.

</details>


### [119] [Experience Memory Graph: One-Shot Error Correction for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13884)
*Wenjun Wang,Yuchen Fang,Fengrui Liu,Zibo Liang,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出经验记忆图(EMG)框架，将LLM智能体的故障恢复重构为图匹配问题，通过将失败轨迹与专家轨迹转化为有向决策图并提取公共子图和图编辑路径来指导单次无循环执行，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上优于现有反思基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在复杂长程任务中容易出现累积错误且难以从失败中恢复，而基于提示的自我纠正机制存在脆弱性、高时延和API成本高、难以泛化等问题。

Method: 1) 将失败的探索轨迹和成功的专家轨迹转化为有向决策图；2) 通过图匹配提取公共子图（成功工作流）和图编辑路径（指示如何添加、删除或重新标记动作）；3) 将提取的修复信息存储为带有任务内节点和跨任务边的记忆图；4) 测试时检索相关记忆，指导智能体单次无循环执行。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld基准上，EMG在成功率和平均奖励方面一致优于最先进的反思基线方法，且无需测试时的试错循环。

Conclusion: 将故障恢复建模为图匹配问题是一种有效且高效的方法，能够避免迭代试错成本，提升LLM智能体在长程任务中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown remarkable capabilities in autonomous decision-making by generating sequential trajectories of states, actions, and observations. However, in complex, long-horizon tasks, these agents frequently suffer from compounding errors and struggle to recover from failures. Existing self-correction mechanisms rely on prompt-based reflection, which is inherently brittle, incurs heavy time and API costs due to iterative trial-and-error loops, and produces task-specific memory that may be hard to generalize to new scenarios. To address this, we propose Experience Memory Graph (EMG), a framework that reformulates agent failure recovery as a graph matching problem. At training time, we convert both failed exploration trajectories and successful expert trajectories into directed action decision graphs. By matching these graphs, we extract common subgraphs (successful workflows) and graph edit paths that explicitly indicate how to correct failures (e.g., which actions to add, delete, or relabel under a given observation), and store them in a memory graph with intra-task nodes and cross-task edges. At test time, EMG retrieves relevant insights and guides the agent in a single, loop-free execution. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that EMG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art reflection baselines in success rate and average reward, while requiring no test-time trial-and-error.

</details>


### [120] [AIMO Interpretability Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13899)
*Michal Štefánik,Philipp Mondorf,Andreas Waldis,Qianying Liu,Chuan Yang,Michal Spiegel,Josef Kuchař,Marek Kadlčík,Adam Vawda-Oomerjee,Chaoran Liu,Simon Frieder,Barbara Plank,Fazl Barez,Pontus Stenetorp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AIMO可解释性挑战赛，旨在通过分析模型内部机制，区分前沿数学语言模型的稳健推理与虚假推理，并构建开放性鲁棒性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推理基准仅关注最终答案准确性，无法揭示模型是依赖稳定的推理机制还是利用脆弱的推理捷径，这一局限性催生了该挑战赛。

Method: 竞赛提供：奥林匹克级数学推理问题及其符号表示（可生成新功能变体）、前沿推理模型访问权限、对抗鲁棒性评估、计算基础设施支持。参赛者需开发方法识别哪些模型能够稳健地解决问题。

Result: 预期产出包括：新的开放性鲁棒性基准、基线系统，以及区分模型稳健推理与虚假推理的方法体系。

Conclusion: 该竞赛将可解释性与泛化研究联系起来，核心科学问题是：能否确定前沿AI模型决策的泛化性及可靠程度，从而为数学推理和可解释性提供标准化基准测试基础。

Abstract: We propose the AIMO Interpretability Challenge, a competition on distinguishing robust from spurious reasoning in frontier mathematical language models based on the models' internal mechanisms. The challenge is motivated by a central limitation of standard reasoning benchmarks: strong final-answer accuracy does not reveal whether a model relies on stable reasoning mechanisms or exploits brittle reasoning shortcuts. Building on AI Mathematical Olympiad (AIMO) problems and submissions, together with resources from the Fields Model Initiative, the competition will provide (1) newly-published olympiad-level math reasoning problems and their symbolic representations, allowing generation of novel functional variants, (2) access to frontier reasoning models, and (3) assessments of models' adversarial robustness on these problems. Participants will use these resources, along with our computing infrastructure support, to develop methods for identifying which models solve problems robustly. Our competition will also create a new, open robustness benchmark and baseline systems, aiming to provide a lasting foundation for standard benchmarking in mathematical reasoning and interpretability. Scientifically, the competition connects interpretability and generalization research around a central question in AI research: can we determine if, and to what extent, the decision-making of frontier AI models is generalizable and thus, reliable?

</details>


### [121] [A Self-Evolving Agent for Longitudinal Personal Health Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13940)
*Haoran Li,Jiebi Deng,Tong Jin,Jinghong Han,Yuxin Wang,Zexin Wang,Qingyi Si,Weikang Gong,Xiahai Zhuang,Jia You,Wei Cheng,Jianfeng Feng,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HealthClaw 是一个开源智能体架构，通过分离共享医学知识与私有纵向记忆，并利用归纳机制实现自我演化，在900个纵向支持探测中将准确率从0.2%提升至45.7%，同时降低71.7%的上下文暴露量，为个人健康管理的长期AI支持提供了有效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的健康AI系统将每次请求独立处理，忽略了个人健康管理具有重复性和连续性的特点，无法适应个体在长期过程中的习惯、偏好、测量指标和风险变化。

Method: HealthClaw 采用开源智能体架构，将共享安全规则和医学知识与私有纵向记忆分离；私有记忆包含个人档案、可复用程序和事件痕迹；每次交互后通过归纳机制决定更新档案、修订程序、保留事件痕迹或排除无关信息，实现记忆的自我演化。

Result: 在900个纵向支持探测中，HealthClaw 的准确率从基线（当前查询提示）的0.2%提升至45.7%，且提示侧上下文暴露量比全历史提示降低71.7%；在100个隐私探测中，HealthClaw 实现了更高的隐私感知回答质量和更少的不安全披露；在9项生物医学任务中，平均绝对增益为27.0个百分点，其中7项经FDR校正后仍显著。

Conclusion: HealthClaw 通过受控的自我演化记忆机制为纵向个人健康智能体提供了有效的离线基准，但临床有效性仍需前瞻性评估验证。

Abstract: Personal health management unfolds over repeated encounters, yet most health AI systems treat each request in isolation. We developed HealthClaw, an open-source agent architecture that updates support as a person's routines, preferences, measurements and risks change. It separates shared safety rules and medical knowledge from private longitudinal memory containing profile facts, reusable procedures and episodic traces. After each episode, induction determines what should update the profile, revise a procedure, remain episodic or be excluded. We evaluated HealthClaw with a synthetic year-long benchmark and nine 200-case biomedical tasks. Across 900 longitudinal support probes, answer accuracy increased from 0.2% with current-query prompting to 45.7% with HealthClaw, while prompt-side context exposure was 71.7% lower than with full-history prompting. In 100 privacy probes, HealthClaw produced higher privacy-aware answer quality and fewer unsafe disclosures than both baselines. Across the biomedical tasks, the mean absolute gain in the task-specific primary metric was 27.0 percentage points, and seven gains remained significant after false-discovery-rate correction. These offline benchmarks support governed, self-evolving memory for longitudinal personal health agents, although clinical effectiveness requires prospective evaluation. HealthClaw is publicly available at https://github.com/HC-Guo/HealthClaw.

</details>


### [122] [Do Agent Optimizers Compound? A Continual-Learning Evaluation on Terminal-Bench 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14004)
*Wenxiao Wang,Priyatham Kattakinda,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体优化方法在持续学习场景下收益是否可叠加，发现仅有内置回归控制的优化方法（RELAI-VCL）能实现正向迁移与持续改进，在终身学习平均通过率上达76.4%，显著优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体优化方法多采用单次静态评估，无法反映实际部署中优化反复迭代的真实场景，核心问题在于优化驱动的收益能否在连续任务中叠加而不侵蚀前期成果。

Method: 基于Terminal-Bench 2.0构建两阶段持续学习评估框架，在同等优化预算下对比GEPA、Meta Harness和RELAI-VCL三种智能体优化方法，测量其在静态设定和新任务引入后的表现差异。

Result: GEPA在新任务上迁移效果差于未优化基线，Meta Harness正向迁移但无法利用第二轮预算继续提升，仅RELAI-VCL实现正向迁移并持续改进，终身平均通过率76.4%远高于其他方法（GEPA 66.0%、Meta Harness 64.6%、基线58.7%）。

Conclusion: 优化收益可叠加的关键在于优化循环中内置回归控制机制，该机制提供归纳偏置以抑制无法泛化的捷径解。

Abstract: Most reported gains from agent-optimization methods are one-shot: an agent is optimized against a fixed benchmark and the resulting improvement is reported as if it were a stable property of the method. This does not test the setting that matters for deployed agents, where optimization is applied recursively as new failures and new tasks appear over time. The central question this raises is whether optimizer-driven gains compound: after an agent has been optimized once, can it be optimized again on newly arrived tasks without eroding the gains the first round produced? We study this question with a two-phase continual-learning evaluation built from hard tasks in Terminal-Bench 2.0, comparing three approaches to agent-harness optimization (GEPA, Meta Harness, and RELAI's Verifiable Continual Learning, RELAI-VCL) under identical optimization budgets. All three methods improve over the baseline agent in the conventional, static, single-phase setting. However, once new tasks are introduced, the methods diverge sharply: GEPA's optimized agent transfers below the unoptimized baseline, Meta Harness transfers well but fails to improve further once given a second optimization budget, and RELAI-VCL is the only method that both transfers positively to unseen tasks and continues improving after those tasks are folded into the optimization objective, reaching the highest pass rate at every evaluated stage and the highest lifelong average pass rate overall (76.4% vs. 66.0% for GEPA, 64.6% for Meta Harness, and 58.7% for the baseline). Our key observation was that optimization gains compounded only when regression control was built into the optimization loop, providing an inductive bias against shortcut solutions that fail to generalize.

</details>


### [123] [AI-accelerated End-to-End Framework for Rapid Professional Upskilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14044)
*Tam Nguyen,Hung Nguyen,Robert Ogburn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端AI加速技能提升框架，覆盖知识获取、内容开发、审核、教学和评估五个阶段，并通过NASBA认证、学员通过NVIDIA认证考试以及构建风险数据集三个外部信号验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 到2030年，每100名工人中有59人需要重新技能培训或提升技能，但企业缩小技能差距的平均时间从2014年的约3天增长到2018年的36天。现有框架大多加速技能提升项目的单一阶段且缺乏行业验证，亟需一个端到端且经过行业验证的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个端到端AI加速框架，涵盖五个阶段：知识获取、内容开发、内容审查与验证、教学以及评估开发，同时关注生产效率和学习效率。

Result: 三个外部验证信号：1）美国国家会计委员会协会（NASBA）审核并批准了基于该框架的技能提升项目，授予继续职业教育学分；2）3名学员通过该框架学习后，在极短时间内通过了NVIDIA认证的Agentic AI专家考试，另有14人正在进行中；3）该项目的知识库支持复杂下游分析，生成了一个包含1,267个风险项的数据集，用于管理多智能体AI系统风险。

Conclusion: 该端到端AI加速框架在提升技能培训效率方面具有显著效果，并通过了严格的行业验证。

Abstract: By 2030, 59 of every 100 workers will need reskilling or upskilling, yet the average time to close an enterprise skills gap grew from roughly 3 days in 2014 to 36 days in 2018. Most current frameworks accelerate single stages of upskilling programs and generally lack industry validation. We present an end-to-end framework that applies AI acceleration across five stages of knowledge acquisition, content development, content review and verification, teaching, and assessment development; with a strong focus on both production and learning efficiency. Three strong external signals validates the framework: the US National Association of State Boards of Accountancy reviewed and approved an upskilling program built on the framework for continuing-professional-education credits; 3 learners followed the program and passed the NVIDIA Certified Professional in Agentic AI exam in a significantly short amount of time, with 14 more in progress; the program's knowledge base supports complex downstream analysis such as the production of a robust 1,267 risk item dataset for managing multi-agent AI system risks.

</details>


### [124] [Earthquaker-AI: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework with Rubric-Based Assessment for Primary School Earthquake Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14046)
*Xanthi Kokkinou,Chaido Mizeli,Nafsika Koulaxidou,Marina Delianidi,Konstantinos Diamantaras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Earthquaker-AI是一个混合教育框架，将基于检索增强生成（RAG）的对话AI助手与Lego WeDo2机器人项目相结合，旨在通过渐进式学习轨迹（从选择题到书面表达）提升小学生的地震 preparedness 意识和危机管理能力。实验评估显示该系统的回答具有高 groundedness 和准确性，且幻觉率低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有的获奖STEM机器人项目基础上，引入AI对话助手来增强学生对地震准备和应急反应的理解，从单纯的机械模拟扩展到认知和元认知处理层面，弥补传统STEM教育在危机管理技能培养方面的不足。

Method: 采用混合教育框架：（1）机器人组件使用Lego WeDo2自动化模拟地震响应，让学生通过传感器和执行器进行具身交互；（2）AI助手基于RAG技术，语义匹配学生查询与官方安全指南，生成准确响应；（3）按照认知发展阶段设计渐进式学习轨迹：低年级（选择题+二维量规）、中年级（动作序列选择+三维量规）、高年级（书面表达+四维量规，包含表达清晰度维度）；（4）基于量规的言语反馈支持自我调节学习和紧急状态下的冷静应对。

Result: 实验评估表明系统的回答具有高 groundedness 和准确性，且幻觉率低，验证了RAG模块在生成安全、准确响应方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Earthquaker-AI通过将机器人实操、信息加工和反思实践相结合，能促进学生的技术素养、自我调节能力和数字化系统负责任使用，为早期危机管理技能的培养提供了有效路径。

Abstract: This paper presents Earthquaker-AI, a hybrid educational framework building upon a previously implemented educational robotics project by integrating a conversational AI assistant based on Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It aims to enhance earthquake preparedness and conscious action among primary-school students. The system extends the award-winning STEM project Earthquaker moving from mechanical simulation with Lego WeDo2 to cognitive and metacognitive processing. The robotics component uses Lego WeDo2 automation to simulate seismic response, letting students interact with sensors and actuators as tangible representations of protective actions. The assistant operates as a guided learning mechanism aligning student responses with safety guidelines, while providing rubric-based verbal feedback that supports self-regulated learning and calmness under emergency conditions. Earthquaker-AI follows a progressive learning trajectory aligned with cognitive development. In early grades, the focus is on basic recognition of safety actions through multiple-choice questions, assessed via a two-dimensional rubric. In middle grades, students identify correct action sequences through multiple-choice questions, evaluated via a three-axis rubric. In upper grades, the approach shifts to verbal production, requiring short written responses assessed via a four-dimensional rubric that includes clarity of expression. The dialogic module uses RAG to match student queries semantically with official guidelines, generating safe, accurate responses. Experimental evaluation shows high groundedness and accuracy, with a low hallucination rate. Overall, Earthquaker-AI combines hands-on engagement, information processing, and reflective practice. Combining robotics, rubrics, and AI promotes technological literacy, self-regulation, and responsible use of digital systems, contributing to early crisis-management skills.

</details>


### [125] [Deep Interaction: An Efficient Human-AI Interaction Method for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14049)
*Hefeng Zhou,Jinxuan Zhang,Jiong Lou,Yuxin Liu,Chaochao Lu,Jingjing Qu,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出深度交互机制（Deep Interaction），允许用户直接编辑LLM的CoT推理错误步骤，通过蒸馏提示引导模型沿修正路径推理，在STEM任务中纠正成功率提升25%以上，token使用减少约40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在CoT推理出现错误时，重新生成可能再次犯错，而人工逐轮纠正效率低且错误易重复出现，缺乏精准修正错误步骤的有效机制。

Method: 允许用户直接编辑原始响应的错误部分，保留正确推理步骤；将编辑后的CoT提炼为蒸馏提示（distilled prompt），引导LLM沿修正后的推理路径生成最终答案。

Result: 在STEM推理任务上，相比基线方法，纠正成功率提升超过25%，token使用量减少约40%。

Conclusion: Deep Interaction提供了一种高效、精准的人机交互纠错机制，既提高了错误修正的成功率，又显著降低了计算开销。

Abstract: The emergence of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the ability of large language models (LLMs) to tackle complex, multi-step tasks. However, when errors occur, current interaction approaches typically involve re-generating another response that may make mistakes again, or users laboriously flag the faulty step in follow-up turns that may get responses <You are right, I made a mistake here> followed by similar errors recurring. To address this issue, we propose an efficient human intervention mechanism for precisely correcting reasoning errors in LLMs, termed Deep Interaction. Our approach enables direct editing of the original response, allowing erroneous parts to be corrected while preserving accurate reasoning steps. We refine the edited CoT into a distilled prompt, which then steers the LLM along the corrected reasoning path. Experimental results show that our method achieves over a 25% improvement in correction success rate and reduces token usage by approximately 40% on STEM tasks reasoning compared to baseline approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Autonomous UAV Route Planning for Coverage Maximization in Environmental Monitoring: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13054)
*Sebastian Jouannet-Contreras,Carola Figueroa-Flores*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报道了一项关于面向覆盖的环境监测中自主无人机航路规划的系统文献综述（SLR）的协议与初步结果，遵循PRISMA 2020框架，从Scopus和Web of Science中检索2015–2026年间的研究，当前阶段已识别562条记录，去除重复后筛选401条，保留247篇进行全文评估，初步分析显示研究集中在覆盖导向、多无人机协调和能量感知优化，而天气、不确定性和障碍密集环境研究较少，多数研究基于仿真验证，存在仿真到现实的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统性梳理面向覆盖的环境监测中自主无人机航路规划的研究现状，识别成熟技术和未解决的差距，以支持现实环境监测任务。

Method: 采用PRISMA 2020框架进行系统文献综述，检索Scopus和Web of Science两大数据库，时间跨度为2015–2026年，重点关注路径规划、覆盖路径规划和信息路径规划，分析算法族、覆盖与能量度量、障碍处理、几何环境表示和环境约束等维度。

Result: 初步检索获得562条记录，去除161条重复后得到401条唯一记录，经标题/摘要/关键词筛选后保留247篇进行全文评估（其中235篇合格，12篇边界待定）。初步分析表明研究集中于覆盖导向、多无人机协调和能量感知优化，对天气、不确定性和障碍密集环境关注不足，多数基于仿真验证，近年强化学习、混合优化和几何感知规划兴趣上升。

Conclusion: 该领域研究活跃但碎片化，需要结构化综合以识别成熟技术和未解决的关键差距，特别是仿真到现实环境的鸿沟问题亟待关注。

Abstract: Environmental monitoring with unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) requires route planning methods that maximize covered area while handling energy limits, operational constraints, and geometric complexity. This paper reports the protocol and preliminary results of an ongoing systematic literature review (SLR) on autonomous UAV route planning for coverage-oriented environmental monitoring. The review follows the PRISMA 2020 framework and searches Scopus and Web of Science for studies published between 2015 and 2026. The protocol focuses on path planning, coverage path planning, and informative path planning, with emphasis on algorithmic families, coverage and energy metrics, obstacle handling, geometric environment representations, and environmental constraints. At the current stage, 562 records have been identified, 161 duplicates have been removed, and 401 unique records have been screened by title, abstract, and keywords. From these, 247 studies were retained for full-text eligibility assessment (235 eligible and 12 borderline records to be resolved during full-text review). A preliminary analysis of the retained studies suggests strong concentration on coverage-oriented formulations, multi-UAV coordination, and energy-aware optimization, while fewer studies explicitly address weather, uncertainty, or obstacle-rich environments. Most retained studies rely on simulation-based validation, highlighting a potential simulation-to-reality gap, and recent publications show increasing interest in reinforcement learning, hybrid optimization, and geometry-aware planning. These early findings indicate an active but fragmented research landscape and support the need for a structured synthesis to identify mature techniques and unresolved gaps for realistic environmental monitoring missions.

</details>


### [127] [HRIBench: Benchmarking Interaction-Centric Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13056)
*Chang Liu,Jiawei Zhang,Tao Zhang,Ye Wang,Hongyu Zhou,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HRIBench是一个面向意图感知的人机协作诊断基准，包含13个角色条件任务和650+评估场景，通过结构化交互脚本建模共享代理下的协作约束。实验表明当前基础机器人策略在时间协调与意图感知行为上存在重大局限，在HRIBench上微调可显著提升物理任务成功率（GR00T N1.5从0.10提升至0.43）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基准主要评估隔离的操作技能，未建模人机交互结构，而真实协作需要对共享代理下的意图理解、时间同步、协议遵守和动态环境安全交互进行协调。

Method: 将协作任务表示为结构化场景脚本，显式建模代理角色、时间依赖、协调约束和人类行为分布；定义Instructor、Collaborator、Intruder三种交互角色；引入同步性、响应性、协议合规性和安全性等可解释交互指标。

Result: GR00T、pi0.5和ACT等基础策略在协作场景中表现不佳，但在HRIBench上微调后协作性能持续提升。真实世界适应实验中，HRIBench生成的仿真数据将GR00T N1.5的物理任务成功率从0.10提升至0.43。

Conclusion: 当前基础机器人策略在协作环境中存在时间协调和意图感知行为的重大局限，HRIBench作为交互中心机器人学习的基准具有重要价值。

Abstract: Current vision-language-action (VLA) benchmarks primarily evaluate isolated manipulation skills while leaving human-robot interaction structure largely unmodeled. However, real-world collaboration fundamentally requires coordination under shared agency, including intent understanding, temporal synchronization, protocol adherence, and safe interaction in dynamic environments. To address this gap, we introduce HRIBench, a diagnostic benchmark for intent-aware human-robot collaboration based on executable interaction scenarios. HRIBench represents collaborative tasks as structured scenario scripts that explicitly model agent roles, temporal dependencies, coordination constraints, and human behavior distributions. Building on this abstraction, HRIBench defines three representative interaction roles: Instructor, Collaborator, and Intruder, covering intent communication, joint coordination, and robustness under human intervention. The benchmark contains 13 role-conditioned tasks with over 650 evaluation episodes generated from diverse interaction trajectories and scene variations. Beyond binary task success, HRIBench introduces interpretable interaction-centric metrics spanning synchronization, responsiveness, protocol compliance, and safety. We evaluate adapted policies based on GR00T, pi0.5, and ACT under a unified protocol. Results show that current foundation robot policies struggle substantially in collaborative settings despite strong manipulation ability, revealing major limitations in temporal coordination and intent-aware behavior. Fine-tuning on HRIBench consistently improves collaborative performance. In a real-world adaptation study, simulation data generated by HRIBench improves GR00T N1.5's physical-task success rate from 0.10 to 0.43, demonstrating the benchmark's value for advancing interaction-centric robot learning.

</details>


### [128] [Power from Potential: A Survey of Electrostatic Actuators for Haptics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13058)
*Ahad M. Rauf,Ran Zhou,Eric Acome,Madeline Balaam,Sean Follmer,Teng Han,Craig Shultz,Daniel Leithinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文聚焦综述高压静电致动器（HVEA）在触觉反馈中的应用，涵盖静电可切换粘合剂、介电弹性体致动器、软电液致动器和电动力学泵四大类，分析其机制、带宽、力密度、空间可扩展性及在穿戴式和地面式触觉界面中的适用性，并指出设计约束、改进策略与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统马达和温敏聚合物因体积大、功耗高，难以满足触觉界面向柔软、轻薄、静音、节能方向发展的需求；高压静电致动器（HVEA）凭借快速、静音、低功耗且在可定制柔性形态下运行的特性，成为有前景的替代方案，亟需系统性综述以指导技术发展。

Method: 采用聚焦综述方法，系统梳理四大类HVEA（静电可切换粘合剂、介电弹性体致动器、软电液致动器、电动力学泵），从工作机制、触觉输出能力、带宽与力密度、空间可扩展性等维度进行横向对比分析，并评估其在穿戴式和地面式界面中实现皮肤觉与运动觉反馈的适用性。

Result: 识别出各类HVEA在触觉渲染中的共性设计约束与瓶颈，总结出在人体工学优化、制造流程简化和自感知集成方面的新兴策略，并明确了HVEA在触觉交互中具有独特优势的应用场景。

Conclusion: HVEA在实现软、薄、静音、节能的触觉致动方面具有独特优势，但仍需攻克多项工程挑战才能从实验室走向实际应用；论文指出了将HVEA转化为实用系统所需的关键研究方向。

Abstract: As haptic interfaces integrate more seamlessly into wearables and everyday environments, they increasingly require actuators that are soft, thin, silent, and energy efficient. However, conventional motors and temperature-responsive polymers often struggle to deliver these properties due to their bulky form factors and high power consumption. High-Voltage Electrostatic Actuators (HVEAs), which generate force by applying an electric field to localized charge concentrations using high voltages and ultra-low currents, have recently emerged as a compelling alternative due to their fast, silent, and low-power operation within highly customizable and compliant form factors.
  This paper presents a focused review of HVEAs for haptics, examining four major classes: electrostatic switchable adhesives, dielectric elastomer actuators, soft electrohydraulic actuators, and electrokinetic pumps. For each class, we describe their mechanisms that enable haptic output; characterize their bandwidths, force densities, and spatial scalability; and evaluate their versatility for rendering cutaneous and kinesthetic feedback across wearable and world-grounded interfaces. Through this cross-technology analysis, we identify common design constraints and emerging strategies for improving ergonomics, streamlining fabrication, and integrating self-sensing. We conclude by outlining where HVEAs are uniquely positioned to advance haptic interaction and highlighting key research directions needed to translate these technologies into practical systems.

</details>


### [129] [GPUSimBench: Towards Scalable and Reliable GPU-Accelerated Simulators in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13059)
*Huzhenyu Zhang,Shenghai Yuan,Wenrui Yan,Li Ma,Hengjie Li,Jingcheng Pang,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GPUSimBench基准，系统评估主流GPU加速机器人模拟器（Isaac Lab、Genesis）在并行扩展性、物理保真度和执行确定性之间的权衡，揭示了GPU批处理执行引入的非确定性及其对可重复性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据驱动具身AI转向大规模并行模拟训练，GPU加速模拟器作为基础数据基础设施，其并行效率、物理保真度和执行确定性之间的权衡尚未被充分研究，阻碍了可靠机器人学习的发展。

Method: 提出GPUSimBench基准，包含三项评估：(1) 物理基础评估——通过控制斜面任务量化模拟动力学与真实世界的分布对齐程度；(2) 并行可扩展性评估——测量不同环境数量下的吞吐量和内存占用；(3) 非确定性量化——揭示GPU批处理执行带来的运行间和环境间变异性，并识别四个随机性经验区域。

Result: 量化了主流GPU模拟器在扩展性、物理一致性和计算确定性方面的隐藏限制，揭示了GPU批处理执行固有的非确定性，表现为在相同初始条件下显著的运行间和环境间变异性，并识别出四个随机性经验区域。

Conclusion: 无约束的并行扩展会损害可重复性，必须施加显式约束才能保证机器人学习的可靠性。

Abstract: Data-driven embodied AI is rapidly transitioning into a paradigm that scales training through massively parallel simulation, where GPU-accelerated simulators serve as the foundational data infrastructure. However, as computational throughput scales, the underlying trade-offs between parallel efficiency, physical fidelity, and execution determinism remain largely unexamined, hindering the development of reliable robot learning. In this paper, we expose the hidden limits of mainstream GPU-based robotic simulators (e.g., Isaac Lab, Genesis) by introducing GPUSimBench, which focuses on scalability, physical consistency, and computational determinism. First, GPUSimBench establishes a physical grounding evaluation with a controlled inclined-plane task, quantifying the distributional alignment between simulated dynamics and their real-world counterparts. Second, we benchmark parallel scalability by measuring throughput and memory footprints across scaling environment counts. Crucially, beyond standard performance metrics, we unveil and quantify the inherent non-determinism introduced by GPU-batched execution, characterized by significant run-to-run and inter-environment variability even under identical initial conditions. Finally, we identify four empirical regimes of stochasticity within current simulator stacks, highlighting that unbounded scaling can compromise reproducibility without explicit constraints.

</details>


### [130] [A Bayesian framework for the uncanny valley in humanoid robot design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13060)
*Shimon Honda,Rin Shibano,Hideyoshi Yanagisawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分层贝叶斯生成模型，将恐怖谷理论的启发式规则转化为可操作的数学变量，通过模拟和人类实验验证了类别模糊性、感知失配和不确定性对亲和力的影响，为机器人外观与行为的算法化评估和优化提供了计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的恐怖谷理论设计准则（如采用类机器人外观、避免过度逼真、减少跨模态失配等）难以用于算法化设计，因为它们未被表达为可操控的数学变量。

Method: 提出了一个分层贝叶斯生成模型，将亲和力表示为后验加权的负类别条件惊奇度，并将恐怖谷机制映射到四个变量：偏离预测的机器人类别均值、跨模态人类相似性不一致性、预测不确定性和观测不确定性。通过模拟和人类实验（使用机器人-人类渐变图像，操控预测不确定性（模糊先验机器人刺激）和观测不确定性（模糊评估刺激））进行验证。

Result: 模拟显示类别模糊性和外观-运动失配会降低亲和力，不确定性会重塑恐怖谷形态。人类实验中，增加观测不确定性减弱了中间人类相似性阶段的熟悉度下降，低预测不确定性提高了类机器人外观的评分。

Conclusion: 该框架将经验性的恐怖谷启发式规则转化为可计算的基础，从而能够以算法方式评估和优化人形机器人的外观与行为。

Abstract: The uncanny valley is a long-standing empirical rule in humanoid robot design: making robots more human-like can reduce, rather than increase, affinity. Yet existing guidelines, such as adopting robot-like appearances, avoiding excessive realism, and reducing cross-modal mismatches, remain difficult to use for algorithmic design because they are not expressed as manipulable variables. Here, we propose a hierarchical Bayesian generative model that operationalizes these guidelines as mathematical design variables. The model represents affinity toward humanoid robots as posterior-weighted negative category-conditional surprise and explains category ambiguity and perceptual mismatch as increases in surprise. It maps uncanny-valley mechanisms onto four variables: deviation from the predicted robot-category mean, inconsistency in human likeness across modalities, prediction uncertainty, and observational uncertainty. Simulations showed that category ambiguity and appearance--motion mismatch can produce affinity reductions, and that uncertainty reshapes the valley. In a human-subject experiment with robot--human morphing images, we manipulated prediction uncertainty using blurred prior robot stimuli and observational uncertainty using blurred evaluation stimuli. Increased observational uncertainty attenuated the decrease in familiarity ratings at intermediate human likeness, whereas low prediction uncertainty increased ratings for robot-like appearances. This framework turns empirical uncanny-valley heuristics into a computational basis for algorithmically evaluating and optimizing humanoid robot appearance and behavior.

</details>


### [131] [A 3DGS-Driven Dynamic Viewpoint and Vibrotactile Framework for Subsea Teleoperation Validated via fNIRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13067)
*Fang Xu,Tianyu Zhou,Ruitong Tian,Md Jahidul Islam,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于ROS-Unity框架的多模态遥操作架构，通过动态自适应视点系统（DAVS，基于3D高斯泼溅优化生成无遮挡外视角）和躯干振动触觉反馈，解决水下ROV在狭窄环境中的视野受限和通信延迟问题。30人受试者实验表明：触觉反馈在低延迟下改善路径保持，而3DGS外视角在0.5-1.0秒延迟下表现最优；fNIRS显示延迟导致传统操作认知负荷过载，而DAVS维持了执行控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服水下ROV遥操作中窄小2D自我中心视野和通信延迟对空间感知与操作性能的根本限制，减少操作员的感官工作负荷。

Method: 构建ROS-Unity解耦架构，将主动空间规划与反应式边界避让分离；提出DAVS（基于连续优化和实时3D高斯泼溅合成无遮挡外视角）；使用躯干振动触觉服编码障碍物距离。在BlueROV2仿真中开展3×4重复测量受试者实验（N=30），结合行为指标和fNIRS脑成像评估三种交互模态（自我中心/触觉/外视角）在四种延迟条件下的表现。

Result: 触觉反馈在低延迟下改善路径保持；3DGS外视角在0.5-1.0秒延迟下显著优于其他模态；fNIRS发现延迟导致自我中心遥操作出现认知脱离效应（工作记忆过载、前额激活降低），而DAVS维持了执行控制。

Conclusion: 空间基础的多模态辅助（特别是3DGS驱动的外视角可视化）能显著提升延迟退化水下遥操作中的操作员表现和认知耐力。

Abstract: Teleoperating remotely operated vehicles (ROVs) in flooded, cluttered infrastructure is fundamentally limited by narrow 2D egocentric views and subsea communication latency. We present a multimodal teleoperation architecture built on a ROS-Unity framework that decouples proactive spatial planning from reactive boundary avoidance. The system replaces static camera feeds with a Dynamic Adaptive Viewpoint System (DAVS), which uses continuous optimization and real-time 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to synthesize an occlusion-free exocentric viewpoint from onboard state estimation. To further reduce sensory workload, a torso-mounted vibrotactile suit maps local obstacle clearance to intuitive haptic proximity cues. The architecture was evaluated in a controlled human-subject study (N = 30) using a BlueROV2 navigating a complex simulated underwater facility. A 3 x 4 repeated-measures design compared three interaction modalities (Egocentric, Haptic, Exocentric) under four communication delays (0.0-1.0 s). Performance was quantified using behavioral measures and functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) to assess task-evoked prefrontal activation. Results show that reactive haptic feedback improves path adherence under minimal delay, whereas the 3DGS-driven exocentric visualization provides superior resilience under severe latency (0.5-1.0 s), significantly outperforming the other modalities. fNIRS further revealed a cognitive disengagement effect: increasing latency during conventional egocentric teleoperation overloaded working memory and reduced prefrontal activation, whereas the proactive spatial context provided by DAVS sustained executive control. These findings demonstrate that spatially grounded, multimodal assistance can substantially improve operator performance and cognitive endurance during latency-degraded underwater teleoperation.

</details>


### [132] [HRO: Hierarchical Room-to-Object Framework for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13072)
*Luyuan Jia,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的层级化“房间到物体”（HRO）框架，用于零样本目标导航任务。该框架模拟人类层级化空间认知，从房间语义逐步定位到具体物体，解决了现有方法探索盲目、语义关联不准确的问题。实验表明，在Gibson和HM3D数据集上，该方法在成功率和泛化性上优于现有基于LLM的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的零样本物体目标导航方法仅将LLM用作平面推理工具直接关联物体或区域，缺乏类人的层级化空间认知建模（从房间语义到物体定位），导致探索盲目性强、语义关联准确性不足，未能充分发挥LLM的常识推理潜力。

Method: 提出LLM驱动的层级化房间到物体（HRO）框架，以从粗到细的方式引导智能体探索并导航至目标物体。该框架模拟人类通过房间级语义推理逐步缩小到物体级定位的认知过程。

Result: 在Gibson和HM3D数据集上的实验验证了HRO框架相比现有基于LLM的方法在成功率和泛化性上均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: HRO框架充分展现了LLM在零样本物体目标导航中的强大潜力，通过引入层级化空间认知有效提升了导航效果和泛化能力。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation aims to enable an intelligent agent to explore and navigate to objects of unknown categories in an unfamiliar environment without specific target training. In zero-shot navigation tasks, pre-trained large models are usually employed to leverage their prior knowledge for guiding the agent's navigation. However, existing zero-shot object-goal navigation methods based on large language models (LLMs) merely utilize LLMs as flat reasoning tools to directly associate objects or regions. They lack the hierarchical spatial cognition modeling of human-like room semantics to object localization, which leads to strong blindness in exploration, insufficient accuracy in semantic association, and failure to fully unleash the common-sense reasoning potential of LLMs. This paper proposes an LLM-driven hierarchical room-to-object (HRO) framework for zero-shot object-goal navigation, which guides the agent to explore and navigate to the target object in a coarse-to-fine manner. Experiments on Gibson and HM3D datasets verify that our HRO framework achieves superior success rate and generalization over existing LLM-based methods, underscoring LLMs' strong potential for zero-shot object-goal navigation.

</details>


### [133] [Worlds in One Demo: A Synthetic Data Engine for Learning Open-World Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13154)
*Lingxiao Guo,Huanyu Li,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WANDA提出了一种从单次人类演示出发，通过合成数据引擎生成大量多样化训练数据的移动操作策略学习框架，实现了长程鲁棒性、空间泛化和跨环境泛化，并支持跨形态零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动操作策略的数据收集范式（遥操作、UMI）在大规模数据下需要大量人工成本，难以扩展。本文旨在最大化每次人类演示的价值，通过可扩展的数据生成突破人工数据收集的瓶颈。

Method: 1）从源RGBD观测中重建背景高斯溅射和机器人-物体交互轨迹作为世界基板；2）将接触丰富的交互片段重排为多种空间配置，利用全身运动规划生成新轨迹；3）提出矫正状态扩展增加机器人和物体状态多样性以提升长程鲁棒性；4）从日常照片生成多样化3D世界实现跨环境泛化；5）通过合成渲染的机器人/物体网格与高斯溅射背景生成逼真观测。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实场景任务中，仅需一次真实演示，WANDA训练的策略就实现了长程鲁棒性、广泛空间泛化和跨环境泛化，并成功在另一种形态的移动操作平台上零样本部署。

Conclusion: WANDA通过合成数据引擎从单次演示生成多样化训练数据，有效解决了移动操作策略学习中数据扩展的瓶颈问题，在泛化能力和跨形态迁移方面展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Learning open-world mobile manipulation policies requires vast data to achieve spatial generalization, long-horizon robustness, and scene generalization. Current prevailing data collection paradigms, teleoperation and UMI, demand prohibitive human effort and cost at scale. To scale beyond the limits of manual data collection, we seek to maximize the value of each human demonstration by scalable data generation. To this end, we introduce WANDA: learning open-World mobile mANipulation from one demonstration via a synthetic DAta engine. WANDA first reconstructs background Gaussian splats and robot-object interaction trajectories from source RGBD observations, as a world substrate for later planning and rendering. It then rearranges contact-rich robot-object interaction segments into extensive spatial configurations, utilizing whole-body motion planning to chain them into new trajectories. To enhance long-horizon robustness, it applies Corrective State Expansion to increase the robot and object state diversity at different stages of mobile manipulation. To unlock cross-environment generalization, trajectories are synthesized on diverse generated 3D worlds from everyday photos. Furthermore, we synthesize photo-realistic observations by compositing rendered robot and object meshes with Gaussian splatting backgrounds. We evaluate our approach on extensive simulation and real-world tasks in various scenes. Experiments show that policies trained with WANDA achieve long-horizon robustness, broad spatial generalization and cross-environment generalization from one real demonstration. Moreover, WANDA naturally supports cross-embodiment data generation, validated by zero-shot deployment on another mobile manipulator with a distinct morphology.

</details>


### [134] [Attitude Estimation Using Inertial and Barometric Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13254)
*Melone Nyoba Tchonkeu,Soulaimane Berkane,Tarek Hamel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用气压计辅助姿态估计架构，通过两种非线性观测器（级联Riccati观测器+互补滤波器、SO(3)×R²上的统一观测器）在GNSS缺失/高加速度飞行条件下增强姿态估计，验证了气压计作为轻量级互补传感模态的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS拒止条件和高加速飞行中，IMU无法区分重力加速度与惯性加速度，导致姿态估计不可靠；传统辅助速度传感器（GNSS、皮托管、多普勒雷达、视觉惯性里程计）可能不可用、不稳定或成本高昂，亟需一种低成本、可靠的替代传感方案。

Method: 提出两种非线性观测器：1) 确定性Riccati观测器级联互补滤波器，在一致可观测性条件下保证几乎全局渐近稳定（AGAS），保持姿态动力学的几何结构；2) 在SO(3)×R²流形上设计的非线性观测器，以IMU为输入、气压计和磁力计为输出，在松弛一致可观测性条件下保证局部指数稳定（LES）。

Result: 通过仿真和实际飞行数据验证，气压计辅助姿态估计在最小传感器配置下提供了轻量级、可靠且有效的互补感知能力，在传统速度测量不可用或退化时展现出实用替代性。

Conclusion: 气压计可作为姿态估计中有效的互补传感模态，在GNSS缺失等恶劣条件下为自主飞行器提供实用的姿态估计替代方案，具有低成本、轻量级的优势。

Abstract: Accurate and robust attitude estimation is a key challenge for autonomous vehicles, particularly in GNSS-denied conditions and during highly accelerated flight. In such conditions, Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) alone are insufficient for reliable tilt estimation due to the ambiguity between gravitational and inertial accelerations. Although auxiliary velocity sensors such as GNSS, Pitot tubes, Doppler radar, or Visual Inertial Odometry are commonly used, they may be unavailable, intermittent, or costly. This paper introduces a barometer-aided attitude estimation architecture that exploits barometric altitude measurements to provide complementary information on the vehicle's vertical motion, thereby enhancing attitude estimation within nonlinear observers on SO(3). The contributions are twofold. First, we design a deterministic Riccati observer cascaded with a complementary filter, ensuring almost-global asymptotic stability (AGAS) under a uniform observability (UO) condition while preserving the geometric structure of the attitude dynamics. Second, we propose a nonlinear observer evolving on SO(3)xR2, which integrates IMU measurements as inputs and barometer and magnetometer measurements as outputs within a unified framework, guaranteeing local exponential stability (LES) under relaxed uniform observability conditions. The proposed approaches are validated using both simulated and real flight data. The results demonstrate that barometer-aided estimation provides a lightweight, reliable, and effective complementary sensing modality for attitude estimation in minimal-sensing configurations, offering a practical alternative when conventional velocity measurements are unavailable or degraded.

</details>


### [135] [Parsimonious disturbance-aware minimum-time planning with parametric uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13312)
*Martino Gulisano,Matteo Masoni,Marco Gabiccini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种用于赛车运动的最小圈时规划（MLTP）框架，该框架同时考虑了状态扰动和参数不确定性，通过空间选择性激活策略在关键路段施加鲁棒约束，并使用MPC作为虚拟测试驾驶员在1000次仿真运行中证明其相比名义规划方案具有更低的失败率和更好的可跟踪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扰动感知框架仅考虑状态扰动，未考虑车辆关键参数（转动惯量、质心位置、空气阻力系数）的不确定性，导致规划参考轨迹在实际参数存在散差时可跟踪性下降。

Method: 在先前扰动感知框架基础上，扩展至参数不确定性建模，对每个赛道点传播短时域随机车辆动力学，基于视界末端的场景收紧轮胎摩擦约束；采用空间选择性、简约的激活策略，仅在关键路段启用鲁棒约束以保持计算可行性；使用MPC作为虚拟测试驾驶员，在1000次随机脉冲扰动和参数散差下评估参考轨迹。

Result: 鲁棒参考轨迹相比名义参考轨迹（无鲁棒性规划）在1000次仿真中失败次数显著减少；在适度增加单圈时间代价的情况下，车辆输入和轴饱和度的信号分散度更小，表明可跟踪性更好。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒MLTP框架能有效提升参考轨迹在实际不确定性环境下的可驱动性和鲁棒性，为赛车运动中考虑多重不确定性的轨迹规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This study presents and validates a minimum-lap-time planning (MLTP) framework for motorsport applications that embeds robustness against both state disturbances and parameter uncertainty. The methodology builds upon a prior disturbance-aware framework that, at each track point, propagates stochastic vehicle dynamics over a short horizon and tightens tyre-friction constraints based on the worst-case scenario at horizon end. We extend the formulation to account for uncertainty in key vehicle parameters: moment of inertia, centre-of-mass position, and aerodynamic drag coefficient. To keep the extended formulation computationally tractable, a spatially selective, parsimonious activation strategy confines the robust constraints to the circuit segments where they are most critical. We demonstrate the improved driveability of the robust references by employing a model predictive controller (MPC) as a virtual test driver. For each reference, the same MPC drives a simulated FSAE (Formula SAE) car over 1000 runs on a representative Barcelona-Catalunya sector, with randomly realised impulsive disturbances and parameter scatter. We compare a nominal reference, planned without robustness, against its robust counterparts. The latter yield consistently fewer failed runs and, at a moderate sector-time cost, show tighter dispersion of key signals (vehicle inputs, axle saturations) around the reference values, evidence of better trackability.

</details>


### [136] [Adapting Generalist Vehicle Models for High-Speed MPC Across Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13319)
*Rwik Rana,Jesse Quattrociocchi,Christian Ellis,Nathan Tsoi,Garrett Warnell,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OptCar是一种将通用前向运动学预测基础模型适配到特定车辆的高效方法，通过历史条件动力学适配模块和有限真实数据+合成轨迹的微调策略，在仅需5分钟真实数据的情况下，将高速（6m/s）越野轨迹跟踪误差降低约55%，并保持了跨地形的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用前向运动学预测模型在适配特定车辆时，往往需要大量真实数据，且容易过拟合到特定地形或驾驶场景，导致跨地形泛化能力下降。现有方法难以在保持泛化性的同时实现高效的专精化适配。

Method: 提出OptCar方案，包含两个关键组件：(1) 历史条件动力学适配模块，将近期状态-动作观测编码为动力学上下文标记；(2) 使用有限真实数据结合环境特定系统辨识生成的合成轨迹进行微调。

Result: 在三种地形及拖车任务中，OptCar在6m/s高速下将轨迹跟踪误差相比微调的AnyCar基线降低约55%；仅需5分钟真实数据即可在道路地形上与使用30分钟数据训练的专精模型竞争，且在地形变化时显著优于后者。

Conclusion: OptCar有效弥合了从通用到专精前向运动学模型的差距，在优化特定车辆性能的同时保留了跨地形泛化能力，为高速越野自主驾驶提供了一种数据高效的适配方案。

Abstract: High-speed off-road autonomy requires precise closed-loop control for a target vehicle while remaining robust across changing terrains. Recent forward kinodynamic (FKD) prediction foundation models suggest a promising path, starting from a generalist model and specializing it to the target platform. However, effective specialization remains challenging, as it often requires substantial real-world data, and models adapted to one setting can still overfit to specific terrains or driving regimes. We present OptCar (Optimized Car), a recipe for bridging the gap from generalist to specialist FKD models that preserves cross-terrain generalization while optimizing performance for a specific vehicle. $\texttt{OptCar}$ introduces a history-conditioned dynamics adaptation module that encodes recent state-action observations into a dynamics context token, and then fine-tunes the generalist model using limited real-world data together with targeted synthetic rollouts from environment-specific system identification. In closed-loop model predictive control (MPC) experiments across three terrains and an out-of-distribution cart-pulling task, the largest gains appear at 6~m/s, the highest speed evaluated and the regime in which slip dominates tracking error. On vegetation and dirt, the most slip-diverse terrain, OptCar reduces 6~m/s trajectory tracking error by roughly 55% relative to a fine-tuned AnyCar baseline, and remains the most accurate even when an unseen cart payload changes the dynamics. With only 5 minutes of real data per terrain, OptCar is competitive on road with a specialist trained on 30 minutes of road data, and substantially outperforms it once the terrain changes.

</details>


### [137] [Design and Characterization of a Limb Encircling Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13337)
*Japmanjeet Singh Gill,Gray Cortright Thomas,Nikko Van Crey,Roberto Leo Medrano,Elliott J. Rouse*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种环绕肢体（Limb-Encircling Actuator, LEA）的驱动布局方案，将执行器置于与肢体轴线正交的平面内，以减少外骨骼在身体自然轮廓外的空间占用。LEA实现了7.5 Nm/kg的连续转矩密度（质量894g），并设计了新型径向轴承布局。尽管转矩密度高，但系统仍难以紧贴身体，表明该方案在实现日常穿戴集成方面仍面临尺寸和几何设计的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下肢动力外骨骼的驱动组件通常置于腿部外侧或腰部/背部，超出身体自然包络，导致设备在日常使用中显得突兀，可能限制社会接受度。本文旨在重新思考驱动几何布局，使外骨骼更贴合身体轮廓。

Method: 探索了一种驱动布局，其中执行器环绕肢体，位于与肢体轴线正交的平面内。开发了Limb-Encircling Actuator (LEA)原型，并使用定制测试平台表征其机电特性。同时提出了一种新颖的径向轴承布局，作为传统大直径轴承的轻量化/低成本替代方案。

Result: LEA实现了7.5 Nm/kg的连续转矩密度，质量为894g。尽管具有高转矩密度和创新布局，系统仍难以紧密贴合身体。

Conclusion: 环绕肢体驱动方案在实现高转矩密度方面具有潜力，但若要真正集成到日常服装中，还需克服执行器尺寸和几何设计方面的挑战。本研究为未来扭矩密集型外骨骼设计提供了见解。

Abstract: Lower-limb powered exoskeletons have demonstrated substantial improvements in mobility and function, but most designs place actuation components lateral to the legs or remotely at the waist or back. These configurations often extend beyond the body's natural envelope, making devices intrusive in everyday use and potentially limiting societal adoption. We posit that rethinking actuation geometry could enable exoskeletons that conform more closely to the body. Here, we explored an actuation layout in which the actuator encircles the limb in a plane orthogonal to the limb axis, potentially reducing its spatial footprint around the body. We developed the Limb-Encircling Actuator (LEA) and characterized its electromechanical properties using a custom-built testbed. The LEA also features a novel radial bearing layout with potential as a lightweight or lower-cost alternative to traditional large-diameter bearings. The actuator achieved a continuous torque density of 7.5 Nm/kg with a mass of 894 g. Despite this high torque density and innovative layout, the system remained difficult to contain close to the body. These results highlight opportunities and challenges in limb-encircling actuation and provide insights into torque-dense exoskeleton designs that could integrate more readily into everyday apparel if challenges in actuator sizing and geometry are overcome.

</details>


### [138] [Safe Overtaking for Autonomous Racing Using Hierarchical Optimization and Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13348)
*Hassan Jardali,Kai Yin,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层自动驾驶赛车超车框架，上层通过混合整数二次规划（MIQP）选择超车拓扑，下层采用非线性Frenet-frame MPC结合离散时间CBF约束确保安全，并利用强化学习策略在线自适应调节CBF衰减参数，从而在降低保守性的同时保持安全性，在不同赛道环境下均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的固定衰减离散时间CBF公式在交互式赛车场景中过于保守，限制了超车性能且需要针对不同赛道手动调参，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 将超车问题分解为两层：高层MIQP解决组合性的超车侧选择问题；底层非线性Frenet-frame MPC嵌入离散时间CBF约束实现安全轨迹控制；同时引入强化学习策略在线自适应调节CBF衰减参数。

Result: 仿真和缩比硬件实验表明，没有任何单一固定衰减参数能在所有赛道上取得一致优异表现，而自适应策略在所有赛道中均获得最高综合成功率和始终可靠的安全-性能权衡，无需逐赛道调参。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层自适应框架有效降低了保守性，提高了对环境变化的鲁棒性，同时保证标称运行下的安全约束满足。

Abstract: Autonomous racing overtaking requires balancing competitive performance with safety under nonlinear vehicle dynamics and real-time constraints. Model Predictive Control (MPC) combined with Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) provides a principled mechanism for certifying forward invariance of a safe set. However, commonly used fixed-decay discrete-time CBF formulations can become overly conservative in interactive racing scenarios, limiting overtaking performance and requiring manual tuning across track conditions. This paper proposes a hierarchical overtaking framework that explicitly separates maneuver-level decision making from safety-certified trajectory control, reducing conservatism while preserving safety. A high-level Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) resolves the combinatorial passing-side selection problem by selecting a feasible overtaking topology, while a nonlinear Frenet-frame MPC enforces vehicle dynamics and safety through embedded discrete-time CBF constraints. This decomposition isolates the combinatorial complexity of maneuver selection from the continuous trajectory optimization. To further mitigate the sensitivity of fixed-decay barrier constraints, a reinforcement learning policy adapts the discrete-time CBF decay parameter online, enabling context-dependent modulation of safety margins without directly controlling vehicle inputs. Simulation and scaled-hardware experiments show that no single fixed decay parameter achieves uniformly strong performance across tracks, whereas the adaptive strategy attains the highest aggregate success rate and consistently strong safety--performance trade-offs without per-track tuning, improving robustness to environment variation while maintaining safety constraint satisfaction in nominal operation.

</details>


### [139] [A Hybrid Sampling-Based Trajectory Planner with Game-Theoretic Guidance for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13354)
*Alexander Langmann,Frederico Pita de Araujo,Mattia Piccinini,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合架构，将博弈论推理集成到基于采样的运动规划器中，通过离线学习的势函数和在线梯度优化生成交互参考路径，以诱导防守性行为（如阻挡），同时避免完整动态博弈求解的计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车需要在极限操控动力学与多智能体竞争场景的决策之间取得平衡。直接在线求解完整动态博弈在计算上过于昂贵，难以集成到高频自主软件栈中。

Method: 基于α-势博弈框架，离线学习势函数捕获多智能体交互；在线运行时，梯度优化动态细化交互参数生成交互参考路径，作为高频采样规划器中的动态成本偏置。

Result: 在Yas Marina赛道的髙保真仿真环境中验证，定性和定量结果均表明该方法能成功诱导阻挡等防守性行为，且计算开销远低于完整动态博弈求解器。

Conclusion: 该混合架构在博弈论策略交互与高效轨迹生成之间取得了有效平衡，适合高频自主赛车应用。

Abstract: Autonomous racing demands planning algorithms that balance vehicle dynamics at the limits of handling with strategic decision-making in competitive multi-agent scenarios. Game theory provides a mathematical framework for modeling these interactions, enabling interactive trajectory planning and strategic behaviors, such as blocking. However, directly solving full dynamic games online is computationally prohibitive and challenging to integrate into robust, high-frequency autonomous software stacks. This paper proposes a hybrid architecture that integrates game-theoretic reasoning into a sampling-based motion planner, combining strategic interactions with robust trajectory generation. Building upon an $α$-potential game formulation, we utilize an offline-learned potential function to capture multi-agent interactions.
